Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [184] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近传感器计算框架，通过硬件流水线实现触觉几何重建，相比主机处理方案显著提升了响应速度（28.3ms vs 169.9ms）并降低了功耗（347mW），实现了确定性延迟和高精度几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器依赖主机处理来重建接触几何形状，这种方式存在功耗高、数据传输延迟和调度延迟不可预测的问题，限制了机器人系统的感知和响应速度。

Method: 设计了一种近传感器计算框架，采用谱Poisson求解器作为全流式硬件流水线。该流水线在166 MHz频率下运行，无需数据相关分支或迭代收敛，实现了确定性延迟，并在FPGA上实现了347 mW的功耗。

Result: 硬件流水线在接收第一个输入像素后35,107个周期（0.211ms）输出第一个深度值；在15种接触几何测试中，重建深度与双精度参考值的差异仅为峰值接触深度的0.17%；基于重建结果的片上决策可在28.3±4.9ms内关闭机器人保护反射回路，而相同执行器的基于主机的回路需要169.9±27.8ms。

Conclusion: 近传感器重建技术能够提供准确、节能且具有确定性延迟的触觉几何信息，其时间尺度适合快速机器人接触响应，为下一代高性能机器人触觉系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [185] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出自由形式的文本思维链(CoT)会因推理未接地、延迟破坏闭环时序以及推理与动作token目标冲突而降解VLA模型的低层控制能力。作者认为VLA需要的是消费接地语言的能力而非生成语言，并提出了一个框架：通过上下文后训练注入结构化感知证据并仅监督动作，同时结合智能体工具使用接口主动查询任务相关信息。该方法在多个仿真基准和真实世界任务中均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型普遍采用行为克隆训练，使用静态图像和固定指令。虽然引入文本CoT推理似乎是自然改进，但作者发现自由形式CoT会导致低层控制退化：推理结果未接地、延迟破坏闭环时序，更关键的是推理与动作token的目标优化冲突导致策略学习叙述而非行动。

Method: 提出框架包含两部分：(1) 上下文后训练——将感知证据作为结构化上下文注入，模型仅监督动作；(2) 智能体工具使用接口——策略主动查询开放词汇检测器、单目深度和视觉-语言模型以获取任务相关信息。数据引擎生成多样化、释义化且证据条件的空间描述，使策略学习解释从未见过的语言。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv、LIBERO仿真基准以及8个真实世界机器人操作任务中，该方法在匹配配置下相比CoT方法持续达到SOTA性能和效率。

Conclusion: VLA模型需要的是消费接地语言的能力而非生成语言。通过结构化上下文注入和主动工具查询，可以有效提升VLA的语言能力而不破坏低层控制，优于自由形式CoT推理方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [186] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出协调多声场方法，通过声辐射力与声流流协同作用，成功解决声场操控从微纳尺度扩展至毫米尺度的效率瓶颈，实现毫米尺度螺旋机器人在受限生物环境中的可控运动，为血管内等生物医学场景的机器人操控提供新解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声场驱动操控虽能提供非接触、无创的微纳尺度物体控制策略，但在受限生物环境中因推进效率不足，难以有效扩展至毫米尺度机器人的操控。

Method: 引入协调多声场方法，利用声辐射力与声流流的协同作用驱动毫米尺度螺旋机器人；通过多物理场模拟捕捉机器人在组合声场下的动力学行为；开展实验验证包括平面导航、倾斜攀爬和垂直运动；进行半自主导航实验和猪静脉血管中的体外测试。

Result: 成功实现毫米尺度螺旋机器人的可控运动，包括平面导航、倾斜攀爬和垂直运动；半自主导航实验表明超声波协同显著提高了机器人机动性；在猪静脉血管环境中的体外测试证实协调声场可支持单向和往复运动，满足生物相关受限条件下的操作需求。

Conclusion: 这些发现为将声学微操控技术扩展到毫米尺度提供了机制性见解，证明了协调声场方法可有效支持毫米尺度机器人的可控运动，为需要多功能、可控机器人移动性的生物医学应用（如血管内导航）奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [187] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出XEWorld测试平台评估世界模型的跨躯体泛化能力，发现当前模型主要依赖视觉相似性而非物理动力学进行泛化，无法实现真正的零样本跨躯体渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件化的世界模型虽然在机器人操控领域表现有潜力，但仅在训练机器人上进行评估无法揭示模型是真正理解物理动力学还是仅仅记忆了视觉模式。需要测试模型能否忠实地渲染它从未见过的机器人。

Method: 提出XEWorld测试平台——一个受控的跨躯体测试环境，通过在物理相同的场景中评估未见过的机器人来隔离躯体变量。系统分析当前模型的架构瓶颈和泛化机制。

Result: 当前模型主要作为2D视觉模式匹配器，泛化由视觉相似性而非物理运动学相似性控制；难以将抽象的数值关节动作转换为连贯的视觉轨迹；无法从静态初始观察中预测动态视觉变化；零样本渲染需要像素空间动作和显式时空对齐；少样本适应会导致对已见躯体的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 当前模型无法将学习的物理动力学应用于新的视觉外观，真正的跨躯体泛化需要在架构上进行创新，实现视觉外观与底层物理动力学的解耦。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are promising learned simulators for robotic manipulation, yet evaluating them exclusively on training robots fails to reveal whether they capture physical dynamics or merely memorize visual patterns. To answer whether a model can faithfully render a robot it has never seen, we introduce XEWorld, a controlled cross-embodiment testbed for world models that isolates embodiments by evaluating held-out robots within physically identical scenes. Our systematic analysis uncovers a shared architectural bottleneck: current models act primarily as 2D visual pattern matchers whose generalization is governed by visual similarity rather than physical kinematic similarity. Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [188] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)
*Niklas Hargus,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出协调多机器人拆卸规划方法CoMuDi，在受限工作空间内通过时空约束传播和ST-RRT*算法实现多机器人协调拆卸，最小化完成时间并避免碰撞，实验证明该方法可高效解决大规模拆卸任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人拆卸任务需要在受限工作空间内进行任务和运动规划，同时协调多个机器人的运动以避免碰撞并提高作业效率。

Method: 提出CoMuDi方法：以机器人团队、装配对象和依赖图为输入，创建抓取、放置和退出等复合任务，通过传播时间约束确保各机器人尽早开始和结束任务；集成时空RRT*规划器（ST-RRT*）使单个任务最小化到达时间，从而最小化整体完工时间。

Result: 对比实验显示，CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*规划器相比RRT*规划器具有更高的成功率且能最小化完工时间；在包含6个装配体（最多49个零件和9个机器人）的场景中，CoMuDi返回的机器人路径表现出较低的闲置时间。

Conclusion: CoMuDi方法能够可靠地解决大规模装配体的拆卸任务，通过协调多机器人运动和时空规划实现高效的拆卸作业。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [189] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SkillMemo框架，通过将长周期演示隐式分解为潜在原子技能并集成到动态情景记忆库中，增强了具身视觉运动模型（DP和VLA）的组成泛化能力，在模拟基准和真实世界操作任务中均达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身视觉运动模型，包括扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作模型，受限于大规模具身轨迹数据集的稀缺性，导致在分布外场景中的组成泛化能力不足，缺乏对可复用技能结构的捕获能力。

Method: 提出SkillMemo框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)基于混合专家架构的专家指导轨迹分割模块，通过学习的门控系数将轨迹隐式分割为不同的技能原语；(2)技能级情景记忆架构，以可检索的键值对形式存储紧凑的技能表示。推理时，记忆库检索最相关的技能原语并与模型当前的门控分布融合，为动作预测提供鲁棒的上下文先验。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实世界操作任务上的广泛实验表明，SkillMemo持续增强了DP和VLA骨干模型，达到最先进的性能，超过了π₀.₅，并展现出对未见任务配置的强组成泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillMemo通过隐式技能分解和基于记忆的检索机制，有效解决了具身视觉运动模型在组成泛化方面的局限性，显著提升了模型在分布外场景中的性能和泛化能力，为实现更灵活、更通用的机器人操作提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [190] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种端到端学习到的本体感知里程计估计器，用于足式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的位姿估计。采用足感知交叉注意力模块自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学特征，不依赖手工设定的接触或滑移阈值。通过仿真训练加随机化策略，并在真实世界部分微调，提高sim-to-real迁移。实验表明在多种地形上显著减少位置漂移，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在复杂地形中行走时，脚与地面接触状态不可靠（如打滑、悬空），传统基于滤波的里程计方法依赖接触检测阈值，容易出错；纯学习方法容易过拟合特定策略且sim-to-real迁移困难。需要一种鲁棒、无需接触阈值的端到端本体感知里程计估计器。

Method: TRACE直接从近期惯性测量单元(IMU)和关节测量历史中预测相对位移、相对旋转和体帧速度。引入足感知交叉注意力模块，自适应地融合IMU和腿部运动学信息。训练采用直接监督，并加入两个物理启发的辅助损失：运动学一致性损失和腿部信息可靠性损失。为减少策略过拟合并提升sim-to-real迁移，仿真训练中加入策略随机化，随后在真实数据上对时间编码器与预测头进行部分微调。

Result: 在多种室内和室外地形上的实验表明，TRACE相比经典滤波法、混合方法和纯学习方法基线，持续减少位置漂移。消融研究验证了训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调的贡献，尤其在不可靠接触和sim-to-real失配条件下效果显著。

Conclusion: TRACE为足式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的里程计提供了鲁棒的端到端学习解决方案，通过自适应注意力机制、物理启发损失和sim-to-real策略，实现了优于传统方法的性能，为足式机器人自主导航奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiRoC框架，将视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型的高层任务规划与低层动作执行解耦，通过强化学习持续优化执行器，并在规划-执行间进行对齐，在多个机器人操作基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的训练方法主要将其优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进展和执行鲁棒的长时程操作；虽然分层方法引入了任务分解，但主要依赖离线演示的有监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效改进执行效果。

Method: 提出分层机器人控制(HiRoC)框架：(1)规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行子目标，提供显式语义指导；(2)执行器通过强化学习持续改进基于子目标的动作生成；(3)在强化学习前将执行器与规划器生成的子目标进行对齐，缓解规划与执行间的分布失配问题。

Result: 在多个多样化机器人操作基准上进行了大量实验，证明HiRoC一致性地优于强基线方法；综合分析验证了分层后训练的有效性以及各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: HiRoC通过分层架构有效解决了现有VLA模型在长时程操作中的局限性，实现了规划与执行的解耦优化，并通过模块间对齐提升协同效率，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了更鲁棒的学习框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [192] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Adaptive-WAM通过质量感知的多出口策略，在视频扩散模型驱动的自动驾驶规划中动态减少计算量，在NAVSIM和nuScenes上达到最先进性能，同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型为自动驾驶提供了丰富的时空先验，但现有世界-动作模型在部署仅需自车轨迹时仍承受迭代生成未来视频的开销。本文提出更基础的问题：执行视频扩散模型的多少部分足以做出可靠的驾驶决策？

Method: 通过控制研究视频去噪时间步和Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 深度，发现规划性能对测试的视频噪声水平不敏感，而强轨迹可从中间层解码。基于此提出Adaptive-WAM：在Wan2.2-5B骨干上附加轨迹扩散头到选定DiT块，使用轻量级轨迹质量评分器在最佳轨迹满足质量阈值时终止推理，否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续到更深出口。避免迭代式无分类器去噪循环和VAE解码，根据轨迹质量动态分配骨干深度。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，自适应单轨迹规划器达到90.8 PDMS；固定出口变体以64个提案达到92.6 PDMS。在NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，为比较的前向视频世界模型规划器中最佳结果。无需目标域微调即可迁移到nuScenes，平均L2误差0.88米，碰撞率0.08%。在A100上，自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，端到端规划延迟平均170毫秒，比固定块15规划器低10%，比固定全深度规划器低47%。

Conclusion: 自适应多出口规划器在保证规划性能的同时显著降低计算开销，实现高效部署。代码将开源。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [193] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合视觉地点识别(VPR)与前馈3D几何(FF3D)模型的拓扑度量框架，通过离线映射和在线粒子滤波实现高效、鲁棒的视觉定位，在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有基于外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉地点识别(VPR)虽然具备良好的紧凑性和对视觉外观变化的鲁棒性，但缺乏足够的度量精度。基于局部特征或神经表示的标准视觉定位(VL)方法虽然度量精度更高，但无法兼顾VPR的优势。因此需要一种能够同时满足紧凑性、鲁棒性和度量精度的综合解决方案。

Method: 提出拓扑度量框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)离线自动映射工具，建立场景中不同位置的姿态-外观交互的拓扑度量模型；(2)在线粒子滤波器，结合里程计信息和地点信念进行FF3D推理。该方法将概率VPR与FF3D度量姿态估计迭代结合，在受控图像集中进行序列外观定位，实现了神经度量估计与概率外观定位的融合。

Result: 在三个已知基准数据集上进行了广泛评估，结果显示该方法相比现有基于外观的方法有显著提升。模块化设计允许描述符提取器和FF3D模型保持可互换性。深入分析表明，序列信念能够有效缓解感知混淆情况下的严重失败问题。

Conclusion: 通过整合VPR的紧凑性和鲁棒性与FF3D的精确度量能力，该方法成功解决了基于外观定位中的精度限制问题。拓扑度量框架为视觉定位提供了一个灵活、高效的解决方案，证明了概率外观定位与神经度量估计结合的有效性，为未来的视觉定位系统发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [194] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IcFuzz，首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim机器人仿真器的模糊测试方法。通过LLM语义阶段分割、多级变异算子和多臂老虎机算法，IcFuzz在代码覆盖率和漏洞检测方面显著优于基线方法，发现了11个漏洞（其中9个被开发者确认或修复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真器（如NVIDIA Isaac Sim）作为具身AI的基础设施，对于安全可扩展的机器人系统开发至关重要。然而，其内在复杂性不可避免地引入了软件漏洞，可能影响仿真可靠性。现有模糊测试方法难以有效测试Isaac Sim，面临上下文感知对象语义、分层仿真控制和庞大仿真状态空间等挑战。

Method: IcFuzz采用三阶段方法：1）基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义阶段分割，将仿真程序分解为捕获上下文感知对象语义的结构化阶段；2）在该信息指导下，设计多级变异算子，在不同分层粒度上系统化地测试仿真器；3）采用多臂老虎机算法自适应调度变异算子，以高效遍历庞大的仿真状态空间。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）IcFuzz的代码覆盖率为基线方法的190%-205%；2）在三次12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃，而基线方法未检测到任何崩溃；3）在约四个月内发现11个漏洞，其中9个被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: IcFuzz作为首个针对Isaac Sim的模糊测试方法，成功克服了现有方法的局限性，通过创新的语义感知分割、分层变异和自适应调度策略，在代码覆盖率和漏洞检测方面取得了显著突破，为机器人仿真器的可靠性测试提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [195] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估视觉语言模型作为零样本控制器的视觉感知能力，发现当前VLM仅能作为有界的、选择性的危险辅助工具，而非一体化的零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多地被用作零样本控制器，但成功的轨迹未必表明决策是基于视觉输入的，因为仿真器动态和保守的动作先验可在缺乏有意义感知的情况下产生有利分数。本研究旨在调查VLM的决策是否真正基于视觉输入。

Method: 采用输入消融测试套件（包括盲图像控制、重复相同输入、车道轴反射、非视觉基线及管道完整性检查），对九个直接动作模型、六个结构化局部VLM及一个探索性VLM-MPC层级进行评估。分析两个实体化和三个仿真器上的32,874次评分调用。使用事后泄漏控制对称性共识守卫从16个校准帧中选择两个模型，并冻结原始视图和反射视图的2-of-4危险投票。在272个留出帧上测试。

Result: 直接控制结果总体为负：恒定慢速策略优于脚本化几何控制器，多个模型图像不变或近乎恒定，识别纵向危险的模型在反射下仍无法转换LEFT和RIGHT。无局部VLM满足纵向与横向联合接地标准。然而，图像确定性正向控制估计前车间距的MAE为0.090米且具备精确镜像等价性，证实刺激物包含足够视觉信息；失败是模块化的而非普遍的。事后守卫在272个留出帧上达到0.954平衡准确率（区间估计95%CI [0.895,0.990]）。嵌套留一集外验证在所有12折中恢复相同模型对和阈值。弃用平局可将已提交平衡准确率提升至0.973，覆盖率为0.824。结合确定性感知保留横向权威，离线模块化重放达到0.934动作一致性并具备精确镜像等价性。

Conclusion: 本研究结果表明，当前视觉语言模型应被视为有界的、选择性的危险辅助工具，而非一体化的零样本控制器。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [196] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prior-SG是一个创新的层次化3D场景图生成框架，通过引入任务驱动和大语言模型生成的先验知识，解决了现有方法在开放空间环境中无法有效工作的限制。该系统将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题，结合实时传感器数据与结构化先验知识，实现了高精度的语义区域分割和零样本任务适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图提取框架通常依赖于纯局部视觉聚类或严格的几何启发式规则（如以墙壁分隔的房间），这些方法在开放式平面布局或任意结构的建筑环境中表现不佳，无法满足自主移动平台在高阶空间推理中的需求。

Method: Prior-SG框架采用任务和先验驱动的途径，将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题。系统通过多尺度、开放词汇特征融合策略，将RGB-D传感器流连续聚合成物理感知的实例图；然后利用大语言模型动态合成的先验图（包含环境结构逻辑期望和任务相关词汇），通过最大后验概率估计推断高级功能语义；最后通过优化融合多源专家信息（视觉、几何、离散对象）与拓扑先验的马尔可夫随机场，解决局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多样化的模拟住宅数据集和大型开放式真实环境中的验证表明，Prior-SG在语义区域分割准确率方面达到了最先进水平，能够无墙体情况下稳健划分远距离功能边界，并独特地提供零样本本体灵活性，使机器人能够根据给定高层任务完全重构其空间划分。

Conclusion: Prior-SG代表了一种突破性的空间理解方法，通过融合实时感知数据、结构化先验知识和多源信息融合技术，为自主移动平台提供了灵活、任务感知的空间推理能力，能够适应不同环境结构和任务需求，是机器人空间认知领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [197] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线遍历控制框架，利用高斯过程隐式表面（GPIS）模型，通过触觉传感在线重建未知几何，并结合热扩散势场实现系统表面覆盖与几何学习的同步进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向检验、清洁、打磨等接触式表面作业，机器人需在保持稳定接触的同时系统覆盖表面。传统遍历控制依赖先验几何或视觉扫描，难以适用于未知或动态环境，亟需一种无需先验信息、能在执行过程中同时完成覆盖与几何重建的在线方法。

Method: 采用高斯过程隐式表面（GPIS）模型，从执行过程中的内在触觉信号学习全局表面几何；为提升在线规划效率，局部以接触点切平面采样的点云近似表面，并迭代拟合至高斯过程，该近似同时作为目标分布与覆盖分布的采样域；利用热扩散类比计算势场，将空间覆盖目标转化为平滑的机器人轨迹，引导遍历探索。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验验证了该框架可同时实现遍历覆盖与在线表面几何学习，重建误差接近真实值，表明其在未知/动态环境下具备实用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该在线遍历控制框架使机器人无需先验几何或视觉输入即可在未知/动态表面上完成系统覆盖，并同步学习表面几何，为接触式作业提供了高效、自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [198] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDP（Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy），一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制框架，通过从演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布来联合预测姿态动作和任务阻抗，无需力传感器即可实现接触丰富的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作需要精确跟踪和机械顺应性，可变阻抗控制可提高任务成功率。虽然可从演示中学习阻抗技能避免复杂建模，但顺应性是力无关运动学数据中的隐藏变量。现有方法从轨迹变化推断顺应性时，这些变化可能反映几何适应而非有意顺应，尤其是在空间布局变化的情况下。

Method: 提出Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP)，一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制框架。采用任务参数化方向感知混合模型(TP-DAMM)从多样化演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，将分布映射为刚度曲线，从而联合预测姿态动作和任务阻抗，无需力传感器。

Result: 真实世界实验表明：VIDP在任务成功率方面显著优于固定阻抗基线方法；与高刚度控制器相比降低了交互力；与低刚度基线相比减少了跟踪误差。

Conclusion: VIDP框架通过从演示中学习可变阻抗控制策略，在不需要力传感器的情况下实现了接触丰富操作任务的鲁棒性能，同时平衡了交互力和跟踪精度。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [199] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计了一种新型触摸屏遥操作界面，通过手指运动直接映射机械臂运动，实现了更精细的速度控制和自然的表面交互。对比研究显示，20名参与者在触摸屏界面上完成任务效率更高（时间减少53.5%）、精度更高（覆盖面积增加6.6%）、超调更小，且认知负荷显著降低（NASA-TLX从52降至43），表明触摸屏界面是一种易于实现且能提升核工业表面接触任务表现的有效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工业中擦拭取样等表面接触任务要求精确的路径和力追踪、障碍物规避以及持续的操作员注意力，而传统操纵杆界面难以有效支持这些需求。因此，研究需要设计一种更直观、精确的遥操作界面来满足挑战性环境中的安全有效操作需求。

Method: 研究设计并评估了一种新型触摸屏遥操作界面，将手指连续运动直接映射到机械臂运动。采用对比用户实验方法，20名参与者分别在触摸屏、传统操纵杆和单点击自主模式三种条件下操作位于异国的Franka Emika Panda机械臂执行模拟表面操作任务。记录运动学、生理和行为数据，综合评估任务表现、认知负荷和操作员信任度。使用NASA-TLX量化认知负荷（0-100评分制）。

Result: 触摸屏界面在任务完成效率上优于操纵杆：完成时间中位数从5.38分钟降至2.50分钟（减少53.5%）；正弦路径覆盖面积从84.1%提升至90.7%；两种路径几何形状上的超调均更低。认知负荷方面，NASA-TLX评分从操纵杆的52降至触摸屏的43（减少9分，17.3%），自主单点击模式最低（31分，较操纵杆减少21分，较触摸屏减少12分）。

Conclusion: 触摸屏遥操作界面易于实现，能在远程操作表面接触任务中提高操作性能并降低认知负荷。研究验证了直接运动映射和集成可视化设计的优势，为核工业等要求高精度表面操作的应用提供了可行的改进方案。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [200] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过模仿学习使机器人获得类人运动能力的框架，通过收集大量手写演示数据并扩展高斯混合模型方法，成功生成被评估为更具类人特征的机器人运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习(LfD)为机器人提供了通过观察和模仿人类动力学发展运动技能的框架，减少了对显式编程的依赖。类人的机器人运动被视为建立信任并实现人机自然协作的关键因素，因此需要开发更有效的类人运动学习方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含数据收集、概率轨迹学习和感知用户评估的框架。收集了来自22名参与者的3,142个手写演示数据集(覆盖52种拉丁字母大小写组合)，记录了平面位置、接触力和时间信息。在传统高斯混合模型(GMM)和高斯混合回归(GMR)方法基础上进行扩展，纳入力和标准化时间维度以丰富人类动力学表示，并适应非连续、多段轨迹处理。

Result: 在21名参与者进行的研究中，生成的轨迹获得了71.50分(SD=22.56)的整体类人度评分(0-100分制，50分为中性点)，表明大多数轨迹被认为是更具类人特征的。参与者确定几何定位和轨迹序列是影响感知的主要因素，并对类人的机器人行为持积极态度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过扩展GMM/GMR方法并引入力和时间维度，可以显著提升机器人运动的类人特性。公开的数据集为开发和改进类人机器人运动方法提供了可复现的基准，推动人机交互领域的发展。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [201] [GeniWorld: A Generalizable Interactive World Model for Robotic Manipulation via Visual Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06332)
*Chenghao Gu,Hanyang Yu,Jingbo Zhang,Haitao Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Jinghe Wang,Hanglei Jin,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeniWorld是一个交互式机器人世界模型，基于预训练视频生成模型，通过URDF渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表示，实现空间定位的动作控制。该模型通过解耦机器人运动学与环境动力学，在仅使用有限固定场景数据训练的情况下，实现了优异的域内性能和对高度随机化未见环境的零样本泛化能力，同时可作为可扩展的策略评估器并提供多样化操作轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂和未见环境中鲁棒性有限，多样化真实环境中的机器人学习和评估成本高且具挑战性。现有的动作条件世界模型存在动作可控性有限和对分布外(OOD)场景泛化能力差的问题，需要开发能够在未见场景中稳健泛化的交互式世界模型。

Method: 基于预训练视频生成模型，使用URDF基于渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表示以实现空间定位的动作控制；通过显式解耦本体运动学与环境动力学来缓解场景过拟合；构建结合高频机器人运动控制的自回归视频预测模型实现闭环控制，支持与机器人策略和人类遥操作的交互。

Result: 即使仅在有限的固定场景数据上训练，模型也实现了优异的域内性能和鲁棒的零样本泛化到高度随机化、未见环境的能力；作为可扩展的策略评估器在环境扰动下保持可靠性；在真实世界演示有限的情况下，生成多样化操作轨迹，提高了下游策略在复杂环境中的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeniWorld通过解耦运动学建模和基于视频生成模型的架构设计，有效解决了机器人世界模型在动作可控性和分布外泛化方面的核心挑战，为低成本、可扩展的机器人策略评估和训练提供了新的解决方案，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies exhibit strong capabilities, but their robustness in complex and unseen environments remains limited. Scaling robot learning and evaluation in diverse real-world environments remains costly and challenging. Action-conditioned world models offer a promising alternative, but they often suffer from limited action controllability and poor generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To this end, we present GeniWorld, an interactive world model for robots that generalizes robustly across unseen scenarios. Building on pretrained video generative models, we use URDF-based rendering to transform numerical actions into visual action representations, enabling spatially grounded action control. By explicitly decoupling embodiment kinematics from environmental dynamics, our model mitigates scene overfitting and facilitates modeling of robot-environment interactions. To achieve closed-loop control, we construct an autoregressive video prediction model integrated with high-frequency robot kinematic control, enabling interaction with both robot policies and human teleoperators. In our experiments, even when trained solely on limited fixed-scene data, our model achieves superior in-domain performance and robust zero-shot generalization to highly randomized, unseen environments. For downstream applications, GeniWorld serves as a scalable policy evaluator that remains reliable under environmental perturbations. Furthermore, even with limited real-world demonstrations, GeniWorld generates diverse manipulation trajectories within the world model, improving downstream policy performance and robustness in complex environments.

</details>


### [202] [A Master-Salve Robot Manipulator for Needle-Based Teleoperation in MRI Chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06354)
*Omar Curiel,Jing-Yuan Huang,Po-Chih Chen,Ji Ma,Qing Dai,Wenqi Zhou,David Lu,Holden H. Wu,Tsu-Chin Tsao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种MR安全的用于MRI腔内腹部介入的主从式机器人操作器，采用流体传动实现2+1自由度主控制器与从动器的运动与力传递，并集成了数字主控制器以提供多模态控制能力。系统支持手动、数字、混合及协作控制模式，并通过初步MRI扫描仪实验验证了在活体猪实验中手动控制模式下的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MRI环境下进行腹部介入手术需要安全且高精度的机器人辅助系统，但现有控制器多采用单一轴向或可切换混合模式，缺乏多模态控制能力，限制了操作的灵活性和安全性。因此，需要设计一种MR兼容的主从式机器人，能够实现多模态控制并支持实时MRI引导下的精确操作。

Method: 设计了一种MR安全的主从式机器人系统，主控制器为人工操作的2+1自由度操作器，通过流体传动将运动和力传递给2+1自由度的从动操作器。系统集成了数字主控制器，提供多模态控制能力。采用高输入阻抗、低泄漏的弹性流体执行器用于远程角度控制，低摩擦石墨活塞缸用于针头插入轴的远程驱动，实现亚牛顿力透明度和亚毫米级运动传递。系统支持手动、数字、混合及协作控制模式，并实现了辅助组织穿透、故障驱动虚拟夹具和运动补偿等协作任务。

Result: 初步MRI扫描仪实验结果显示，该机器人系统在床旁手动控制模式下功能可行，能够为活体猪实验提供操作支持。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MR安全主从式机器人系统通过流体传动和多模态控制设计，实现了MRI引导下腹部介入手术的安全性和精确性，支持多种控制模式及协作任务，初步实验验证了其在活体实验中的可行性，为未来临床应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [203] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DyPES-VLA是一种跨具身VLA模型，通过未来预测目标学习共享动力学先验，并利用具身特定的MoE动作头直接在原生动作空间中生成可执行控制，无需手动预处理对齐异质动作，在仿真和真实世界评估中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型已成为机器人操作的有效范式，但为异构机器人具身训练单一通用策略仍是未解决问题。现有方法存在两大局限：(1)未能充分利用跨多样视觉和交互数据共享的动力学先验，限制了跨具身迁移；(2)需要大量手动预处理将具身特定动作转换为通用格式。

Method: 提出DyPES-VLA，包含两个关键组件：(1)通过跨具身数据训练未来预测目标，学习共享动力学先验，驱动共享查询表示捕获物体运动、接触和交互引起的场景变化；(2)具身特定Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)动作头将共享动力学先验翻译为原生动作空间中的可执行控制，共享注意力层捕获共同时间动作结构，具身特定前馈专家解决不同具身的独特运动学约束和控制语义。

Result: 在LIBERO上成功率达98.0%，RoboCasa-GR1上达59.25%，RoboTwin~2.0上达89.02%，在仿真和真实世界评估中均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: DyPES-VLA通过有效学习共享动力学先验和具身特定控制，克服了现有方法的局限性，实现了强大的跨具身泛化能力，无需手动对齐异质动作。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [204] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ω-0，一种用于真实人形机器人并行走动-操作的潜在预测全身世界-动作模型。该模型支持多种视觉输入类型，通过耦合潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成，在11项家庭任务上表现出优于基线方法的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形家庭任务通常需要同步执行行走与操作，即机器人必须作为单一协调行为同时移动、调整姿态、保持平衡和操作物体。然而，现有的人形策略通常将行走和操作分解，而近期的世界-动作模型要么以手臂为中心，要么以视频为中心，缺乏对全身协调行为的有效建模。

Method: ω-0模型接收语言指令、当前视觉观察和机器人本体感受状态作为输入，直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜变量用于真实机器人执行。不同于重建未来视频，ω-0学习紧凑的未来观察嵌入作为轻量级预测目标，将潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成耦合。模型支持自我中心RGB、外部中心RGB和外部中心深度输入，并利用基于控制器的模拟重放将人类/公共视觉-运动先验 grounding 到机器人可执行的动作潜变量中。研究团队还收集了ω-HOME数据集，包含40+小时真实世界家庭人形机器人数据，包括同步的多视角观察、全身SMPL运动、机器人状态和动作潜变量。

Result: 在11项家庭任务上的真实世界实验表明，单一的ω-0模型能够产生平滑的移动中操作行为，并持续优于代表性的模仿学习、VLA、人形机器人和WAM基线方法。

Conclusion: ω-0模型成功地实现了对人形机器人并行走动-操作任务的有效建模，通过创新的潜在预测方法在真实世界场景中展现出优越的性能，为复杂家庭任务的人形机器人应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Agentic Nesting"的多智能体协作框架，通过将企业现有应用封装为自治AI智能体，构建分层嵌套架构来解决企业系统异构性、数据孤岛和流程碎片化问题，并提出了"应用即智能体"集成范式和"对话即集成"交互理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营广泛依赖多种异构业务系统，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。虽然企业投入大量资源建设这些应用，但有效利用和编排它们仍然极具挑战性。传统的企业应用集成方法（如ESB、API网关、RPA）存在固有局限性：高架构耦合、运维成本不断上升以及智能能力有限。

Method: 提出了Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架，核心方法包括：(1)将现有企业应用封装为自治AI智能体，构建分层嵌套结构；(2)从每个遗留应用中提取数字智能体代理，实现自然语言交互和自主操作；(3)通过中央协调器协调多个智能体，进行任务分解和动态调度；(4)提供统一的对话式接口，实现跨应用查询和流程编排。智能体按照反映企业生态系统组合复杂性的分层管理拓扑进行组织，而非扁平化互连。

Result: 提出了两个主要贡献：(1)"Application-as-Agent"集成范式，将传统应用转化为智能体；(2)"Conversation-as-Integration"交互理念，通过自然语言对话实现系统间集成。框架验证了在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中该方法的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting框架为现代企业应用集成提供了新的解决思路，通过将传统系统集成问题转化为智能体协作问题，显著降低了集成复杂度，提高了系统灵活性和智能化水平。该方法具有良好的可扩展性和泛化能力，可应用于复杂的企业生态系统协调和大规模数据应用场景。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [206] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"点火指数"(Ignition Index)，一个用于衡量Transformer语言模型中信息传播方式的定量指标。通过拟合S形曲线来捕捉模型各层对输入信号的响应陡峭程度，验证了全局工作空间理论中的"全或无点火"假设。研究在11个模型上验证了该指标的有效性，发现前馈Transformer在点火特性上显著优于状态空间模型(SSMs)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在将认知神经科学中的全局工作空间理论(GWT)核心预测——"全或无点火"现象——操作化为Transformer语言模型中可量化的机制性可解释性指标。GWT认为意识访问的特征是信息处理的突发式转变，而非渐进式累积。研究团队希望建立GWT动力学预测与神经可塑性机制之间的定量桥梁，以理解不同架构家族中信息传播的本质差异。

Method: 研究者开发了一个四参数S形函数拟合方法，将每层线性探针准确率作为输入信号强度的函数进行建模。核心提取参数为beta-hat(陡峭度)，高值表示接近点火的突变转换，低值表示渐进式构建。研究在跨越五个架构家族的11个模型上进行验证，采用打乱标签对照实验来证明指标对真实语言结构相对于虚假探针容量的选择性(p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U检验)。

Result: 主要发现：(1)前馈Transformer的聚合beta-hat值比SSMs高89%(p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52)，Mamba模型呈现近线性剖面，符合缺乏全局广播的特征；(2)Huginn-3.5B模型沿迭代轴的点火强度是其深度轴的2.12倍，表明循环架构在循环维度上表现类似工作空间的转换；(3)Pythia-410M模型在训练步256时检测到PELT相位转变(+67%)，先于归纳头形成；(4)将点火与模型规模和信号强度相关联的假设未获证实，暗示Transformer架构可能已饱和可用的点火机制。该指标展示了9.6倍的测量选择性。

Conclusion: 点火指数首次为GWT的动力学预测与机制可解释性之间提供了经过验证的定量桥梁，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和前所未有的架构级可辨别性。研究揭示了模型架构对信息处理转换方式具有根本性影响，前馈架构表现出更接近意识点火的突发性特征，而循环架构则在时间维度上展现类似特征。这为理解大语言模型的信息传播机制提供了新的定量工具，并为神经科学理论与人工智能实践的交叉研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [207] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在推理任务中常因中间步骤的局部推理错误而失败，而非全局能力不足。本文提出"啄木鸟蒸馏"方法，通过弱模型生成修正补丁的对比学习信号，将其蒸馏到强模型中，在数学推理基准上显著提升了强模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然具备解决推理任务的能力，但在实际表现中经常失败。研究发现，这些失败主要源于中间推理步骤中的局部推理错误（reasoning bugs），而非模型整体能力不足。这些局部错误往往是可修复的，但直接微调修复轨迹的效果不可靠，说明有用的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于干预如何重塑模型的推理分布。

Method: 提出"啄木木鸟蒸馏"（Woodpecker Distillation）框架：1）使用弱探测模型在相同强模型推理前缀后生成短补丁；2）对比同一前缀下成功和不成功补丁的未来token预测；3）从这些对比中构建修正性教师分布；4）将该分布信号蒸馏到强模型中。这种方法避免了直接模仿补丁或修复轨迹的局限。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试上的实验表明，啄木鸟蒸馏方法持续提升了强模型性能，并优于直接模仿基线方法。实验验证了对比式局部干预蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的推理失败主要是局部问题而非全局缺陷。通过啄木鸟蒸馏方法，能够有效地从弱模型的对比干预中学习修正信号，并将其传递到强模型中，从而改善推理性能。这一方法揭示了修正效果的关键在于干预对模型未来推理分布的重塑，而非干预文本的直接内容。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [208] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索将机器学习模型中的连续预测变量替换为基于临床指南的分类编码，以改善模型解释性与临床医生推理的一致性，同时保持预测性能。在多中心欧洲注册研究中，基于指南的分类编码在三个治疗队列中的两个保持了与连续变量相当的预测准确性，但在一个队列中显著下降。特征重要性排序保持一致，表明分类化编码是卒中预后模型的设计可行选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中的90天结局预测方面具有很强的预测准确性，但由于模型解释与临床医生推理之间的不匹配，临床采纳受到限制。受临床用户研究启发，该研究探索能否用临床指南对齐的截断值替换连续预测变量，从而提高模型解释的临床相关性。

Method: 研究基于一个多中心欧洲卒中注册研究，按治疗方式分为三个队列。研究者将标准梯度提升模型与完全分类编码的梯度提升模型进行比较，后者使用基于卒中治疗指南的特定截断阈值对连续预测变量进行分类。通过统计检验比较两种模型的预测性能差异。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类编码的模型在两个队列中与连续变量模型在统计学上无显著差异，但在一个队列中预测准确性显著下降。然而，所有治疗组的特征重要性全局排序保持一致，表明分类化编码保留了预后因素的核心层次结构。

Conclusion: 基于临床指南的连续变量分类化编码是卒中结局预测模型的一个可行设计选择。虽然在某些治疗组中可能导致预测性能下降，但其保持了特征重要性排序的一致性，同时提高了模型解释与临床推理的对齐程度，有助于促进机器学习模型的临床采纳。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [209] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKILLTRACE框架，用于检测LLM智能体技能（混合模态包）的复用问题。通过提取表达式、实现、操作三个溯源轨迹，其中操作轨迹用技能操作图表示。在基准测试中取得AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898的高性能，并在大规模实际审计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体生态系统中可复用技能的增长，技能复用审计面临新挑战。现有克隆检测工具主要针对单一模态源代码或整体包相似性，而技能复用证据分散在文本描述、代码片段和操作结构中，传统方法会遗漏仅保留部分内容的复用行为。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三种溯源轨迹：表达式轨迹、实现轨迹和操作轨迹。将操作轨迹表示为技能操作图（SOG），捕获激活、流程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取阶段辅助操作轨迹提取；审计时确定性地比较缓存轨迹，针对同功能严格负样本校准每个轨迹，并报告支持复用决策的轨迹。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH上（820个变换后的复用正样本、100个市场锚点和751个负样本控制），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。在包含36,446个技能的实际审计中，基于轨迹归因的证据在仓库级基线之外发现了可操作的复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE有效解决了现有工具无法检测的混合模态技能复用审计问题，通过基于轨迹归因的证据提供可操作的复用审查能力，为LLM智能体生态系统的技能复用监控提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [210] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则(AECR)框架，将具体因果对转化为抽象因果逻辑，显著提升了低频和未见事件组合的因果推理泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件中心的智能分析系统严重依赖因果事件知识，但现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，限制了风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解等应用场景的有效性。

Method: 提出关系级因果抽象范式AECR，设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳(CACI)系统结合相似性约束聚类从噪声因果数据中提取可信AECR，构建两个完整的AECR知识库；同时提出抽象规则指导因果注意力编码器(AR-GCAE)，通过规则指导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入到因果图事件预测(CGEP)基准任务中。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，在稀有和未见事件样本上获得最显著的增益。

Conclusion: 抽象因果知识方法能够有效解决事件因果推理中的泛化问题，为智能分析系统提供更强有力的因果知识支撑，特别是在处理低频次和未见事件类型时展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [211] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一款1530万参数的人类象棋AI，通过将棋局建模为时间感知的序列过程（而非孤立位置），结合棋局历史编码器和时限控制模块，在6.1B个位置数据上训练，以远少于现有模型的参数量实现了更优的人类走棋预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有象棋AI模型通常将每个棋局位置作为独立单元处理，忽略了棋局的时间维度和序列特性。研究者提出，将象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动能够更好地模拟人类决策过程，包括开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向等因素。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号：(1)走棋历史编码器，基于最近20步棋局进行条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向；(2)时限控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型在6.1B个位置（来自1.17亿个Lichess快棋对局）上进行训练，仅使用单张T4 GPU，耗时30天。

Result: Otter实现55.23%的Top-1和90.95%的Top-5走棋预测准确率，超越了先前最先进的人类象棋模型Maia 2，且使用更少参数和训练数据。在11个Elo等级段（<1100至≥2000）中，1900-1999段准确率最高达57.38%。

Conclusion: 将象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动比仅基于位置的方法能产生更准确的人类走棋预测，同时可以使用更小的模型。研究结果证明了时序建模在人类行为模拟中的有效性。代码、训练模型和完整训练日志已公开。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [212] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对深度搜索智能体错误诊断困难的问题，提出SearchAuditBench基准与SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，利用专家标注的大规模失败轨迹数据评估并提升LLM审计器的错误定位、归因与修复能力，实验表明其显著超越现有基线，并增强智能体的错误恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体通过长程网页交互处理复杂问题，但该过程脆弱且复杂：微小的推理错误可能在长而嘈杂的轨迹中传播，最终生成流畅却错误的答案。诊断此类失败困难，需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，可能超出人类能力。

Method: 作者提出SearchAuditBench基准，包含1243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K token），来自8个开放权重模型在5个深度搜索基准上的专家标注数据，每条标注了关键错误步骤、搜索特异的根本原因及修复参考与评分标准；同时提出SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决实现失败定位、归因与修复。

Result: 实验显示，即使最强基线（如GPT-5.5）端到端通过率仅为26.6%，而SearchAuditor在各类前沿模型上均优于所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，且通过其修复重启动失败运行可使智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench与SearchAuditor有效降低了人工诊断深度搜索智能体失败的负担，通过更准确的错误定位、归因与修复，显著提升了智能体的错误恢复能力与端到端通过率。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [213] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入来自自动语音识别和强制对齐的时间对齐音素token，以及语言融合模块，解决了3D高斯溅射（3DGS）说话人头部渲染中的“漏嘴”伪影问题，该方法仅使用单目视频进行图像重建和唇部地标监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS能够实现快速、照片级真实的说话人头部渲染，但准确的唇部动作仍然难以实现：嘴部运动往往过度平滑，可能违反双唇闭合等硬约束，导致“漏嘴”伪影。关键难点在于，从连续声学嵌入下回归目标推断短暂、离散的发音事件，导致预测偏向于平均化的嘴部构型。虽然现代自监督语音编码器提供丰富的韵律和音素线索，但缺乏明确的、帧对齐的语言目标来可靠区分闭合级事件。

Method: 提出音素驱动的高斯溅射（PD-GS），通过ASR和强制对齐流程获取时间对齐的音素token，增强3DGS说话人。核心组件是语言融合模块（LFM），通过学习门控自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保持平滑音频驱动动态的同时加强音素在发音关键段落的指导。PD-GS仅使用单目视频训练，采用图像重建和唇部地标监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS取得了相比基准方法最优的唇部几何（LMD 2.66），并在具有挑战性的音素序列中定性减少了闭合违例，生成更符合语言学的神经化身。

Conclusion: PD-GS通过引入显式的音素级语言目标，提高了唇部动作准确性和减少伪影，证明了音素驱动指导在真实说话人合成中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [214] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多轮智能体特权同策略蒸馏中存在的状态-参考不匹配问题，提出状态匹配路由与上下文自蒸馏方法(SMRC-SD)，通过在每一步验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中支持的状态匹配，仅在匹配状态应用蒸馏，并构建状态条件化教师上下文，从而在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权同策略蒸馏允许同步教师访问训练期参考(如成功轨迹)对每个学生响应进行重新评分，提供密集监督。但在交互环境中，学生的先前行动持续改变执行状态，当学生采取不同行动或以不同顺序完成子目标时，其展开过程可能到达参考未覆盖的状态，使参考成为对实际到达状态的不可靠指导源。无差别应用特权蒸馏因此产生状态-参考不匹配问题，需要为学生的当前执行状态提供兼容的特权参考指导。

Method: 提出状态匹配路由与上下文自蒸馏(SMRC-SD)，显式确定特权轨迹何时以及如何指导同策略学生。在每个回合，SMRC-SD验证学生的当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中支持的状态匹配，仅在匹配状态应用蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的回合。对于每个匹配状态，SMRC-SD进一步从成功轨迹构建状态条件化教师上下文，使监督扎根于实际到达的状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD持续优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，在ALFWorld上将任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，在WebShop上从0.574提升至0.693。控制路由和上下文消融实验支持选择局部支持的回合和构建状态兼容教师上下文对性能提升的贡献。代码见https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD。

Conclusion: 通过状态匹配路由和上下文条件化教师指导，SMRC-SD有效解决了特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题，在交互式多轮任务环境中实现了更可靠的监督信号传递和显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [215] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 小规模认知微调模型可作为心理学实验的噪声天花板估计器。分布内性能几乎不受规模影响（0.6-1B参数即可匹敌70B基线），但分布外泛化需更大规模。模型主要依赖实验刺激和反馈内容（屏蔽后丧失75.7%信息），而非单纯利用选择历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在人类行为数据上微调后可作为通用认知代理，但需要多少规模、模型是处理任务结构还是利用统计捷径仍存在未解问题。研究旨在探究规模效应及模型使用的信息来源。

Method: 在Psych-101数据集（160个实验、1070万试验级选择）上训练14个模型（1.35M-14B参数，跨4种架构）。执行两项诊断：1）逐步屏蔽4个提示通道（任务说明、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）并在27个实验中验证；2）对试验顺序进行排列。同时评估分布内和分布外泛化性能。

Result: 1. 分布内：规模影响甚微，模型性能落在狭窄区间内，0.6-1B参数在保留参与者上可匹敌70B基线。2. 分布外：出现更陡峭的标度梯度，更大模型在泛化到新颖任务结构方面具有明显优势。3. 屏蔽刺激和反馈内容会破坏75.7%的已学习信息并使模型低于随机水平，证明仅选择历史无法解释性能。4. 排列测试显示：独立试验任务具有不变性，但试验顺序由先前反应决定时表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型在作为心理学实验噪声天花板估计器方面显示出前景，但其应用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。规模在分布内几乎无用，但在分布外泛化中至关重要；模型主要依赖刺激和反馈的语义内容而非统计捷径。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions.
