### [305] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹的参数化剖析方法，评估视频事件计数能力。通过三种受控任务（弹球、眨眼、状态转换）发现：模型在事件表示影响证据访问的情况下呈现阶段性时序失败；高频高计数场景下正确率极低；增加采样帧虽提升最终分数但事件恢复不忠实。该方法将视频评估从聚合准确率转向详细诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实视频基准测试覆盖率广但固定片段混淆事件数量、频率、时长和视觉复杂度，难以隔离失效模式；程序化基准虽控制更好但仅评分最终答案，未对报告事件与可执行真值进行审计。

Method: 引入基于轨迹的参数化剖析方法，针对三种受控视频任务进行事件计数评估：弹球撞墙、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换。在2190个视频中，控制渲染不变，改变事件数量N和频率F。每个视频包含可执行事件轨迹用于能力表面估计和时间戳级评估。

Result: 发现阶段性时序失败：在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash能可靠计数持久状态转换（最多12个事件，0.5和1.0Hz），但对瞬时眨眼事件无可靠正计数区域。事件表示决定模型是否能初始访问证据，这一限制随计数和频率增加而加剧。在高计数高频区域，仅0.2%最终计数正确，模型仅恢复18.1%真实事件。增加采样率虽将弹球准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但报告序列与真值一致仅3.7%。额外帧可能虚增最终分数而不产生忠实事件恢复。不同提示策略收益有限，真实视频评估同样显示成功集中于低事件计数。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹的剖析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向对时序推理失败点的详细诊断。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [306] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多智能体系统的自动化心衰电子健康记录特征工程方法，通过证据链接和规范驱动的流水线，在模拟数据上验证了其可行性，并显著提升了表型分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录特征工程是临床研究和人工智能的主要瓶颈，尤其在心衰领域，现有基于规则和大型语言模型的方法仅能部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性有限。

Method: 开发Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，一个证据链接、规范驱动的流水线，用于自动化心衰特征工程；在来自九张EHR源表的500条模拟患者记录上评估，生成结构化特征和聚合特征，并进行结构完整性、规范合规性和来源验证。

Result: nMAS生成132个结构化特征和70个规范评分聚合特征，经验证符合结构和规范要求；添加聚合特征后，HFrEF和HFpEF表型分类的AUROC分别提升；独立LLM规范评估特征证据支持和方法论可靠性得分达81.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明自动化、可审计的复杂心血管EHR数据特征工程具有可行性，但评估限于单机构队列，需要外部验证。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [Multi-Target Micro-Motion Parameter Estimation using MIMO-FMCW Radar with Limited Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05216)
*Chandrashekhar Rai,Sanjay J. Alex,Arpan Chattopadhyay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压缩感知的微动参数估计方法，利用稀疏阵列MIMO雷达和随机LFM波形，通过压缩感知算法实现旋转部件（如UAV螺旋桨）微动参数的高效估计，并扩展到多目标场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统雷达检测小尺寸UAV等带旋转部件的目标需要大量测量数据，存在计算复杂度高、采样需求大的问题。本文旨在通过压缩感知技术减少测量次数，提高UAV检测效率。

Method: 采用MIMO FMCW雷达结合随机间距稀疏线性阵列，发射随机LFM波形实现慢时间域随机采样。首先估计目标的距离、速度和到达角识别整体运动，然后构造3D点目标响应并从总回波中减去以提取微多普勒特征。使用1D正交匹配追踪算法在压缩感知框架下处理残余信号，通过参数化字典联合估计螺旋桨旋转频率和叶片长度。方法还扩展到多目标场景。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在慢时间和空间维度测量数据有限的情况下，能够准确估计微动参数，验证了其在UAV检测应用中的潜力。估计的参数还被用于基于决策规则的分类框架，成功区分不同UAV运动类型。

Conclusion: 提出的压缩感知方法能够有效减少UAV微动参数估计所需的测量数据，在稀疏阵列和有限采样条件下实现准确估计，为UAV检测和分类提供了一种高效的技术方案，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: This work presents a compressive sensing-based approach for estimating the micro-motion parameters of targets with rotating components, such as small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with propellers, using fewer measurements than conventional methods. A multiple-input-multiple-output (MIMO) frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar employing a randomly spaced sparse linear antenna array is utilized. Random sequences of linear frequency-modulated (LFM) chirps are transmitted to enable random sampling in the slow-time domain. At first, the range, velocity, and angle of arrival (AoA) of the targets are estimated to identify the bulk motion. A three-dimensional point target response (3D-PTR) is then constructed using the estimated parameters and subtracted from the total radar return to extract the micro-Doppler signatures associated with target rotation. These residual signals are processed within a compressive sensing (CS) framework using the one-dimensional orthogonal matching pursuit (1D-OMP) algorithm to jointly estimate the propellers' rotation frequencies and blade lengths with the help of a parametric dictionary. The proposed approach is also extended to a multi-target scenario. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach accurately estimates micro-motion parameters with limited measurements in the slow-time and the spatial dimensions, validating its potential for UAV detection applications. These estimated parameters are then used to distinguish among various UAV motion types using a classification framework based on a set of decision rules.

</details>


### [308] [Channel Map-Based Channel Estimation for Near-Field UM-MIMO with Movable Planar Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05520)
*Shuaifei Chen,Cheng-Xiang Wang,Chen Huang,Qianze Yang,Xiping Wu,Yunfei Chen,El-Hadi M. Aggoune*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对超大规模MIMO系统中可移动天线架构带来的信道估计挑战，提出了一种基于信道图谱的统一信道估计框架，通过几何感知LoS估计和高效NLoS恢复实现精度与复杂度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模MIMO系统中的近场传播和高维空间信道对信号处理构成重大挑战，可移动天线架构因几何相关信道变化进一步增加了估计复杂度。现有方法难以在精度和复杂度之间取得平衡，需要利用环境相关传播结构实现高效估计。

Method: 提出基于信道图谱的统一框架，整合可移动平面阵列重构和近场球面波建模；设计结合粗用户位置信息与Fisher信息指导天线放置策略的LoS估计器；提出两种NLoS估计方法：基于草图的降维估计器和利用散射体位置信息的信道图谱估计器；引入可视区域建模和基于结构相似度的多用户导频分配策略。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的基于信道图谱框架相比无信道图谱的先进基准，在估计精度、计算开销减少和可扩展性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于信道图谱框架成功解决了超大规模MIMO可移动天线系统的信道估计难题，通过几何感知建模和多级估计策略实现了精度与效率的最优平衡，为下一代通信系统提供了高效信道估计解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate channel estimation is essential for coherent transmission in ultra-massive multiple-input multiple-output (UM-MIMO) systems, where near-field propagation and high-dimensional spatial channels impose substantial signal processing challenges. Movable antenna architectures increase the estimation complexity further due to geometry-dependent channel variations. Existing approaches struggle to balance accuracy and complexity, motivating the use of environment-dependent propagation structures for efficient estimation. To this end, this paper proposes a unified channel map-based channel estimation framework for UM-MIMO systems, which integrates movable planar array reconfiguration and near-field spherical-wave modeling to support geometry-aware line-of-sight (LoS) estimation and efficient non-LoS (NLoS) recovery. A channel map-based LoS estimator is proposed combining coarse user position information with a Fisher information-guided antenna placement strategy. Two efficient NLoS estimation methods are also presented, including a sketch-based reduced-subspace estimator for low-complexity processing and a channel map-based estimator that leverages scatterer location information for near-optimal performance. The framework further incorporates visibility-region modeling and a structural similarity-based pilot assignment strategy for multi-user scenarios. Simulation results show that the proposed channel map-based framework improves estimation accuracy, reduces computational overhead, and enhances scalability compared with state-of-the-art benchmarks without channel maps.

</details>


### [309] [Lyapunov-Based Completion-Aware Scheduling for PDU Set-Based Real-Time XR Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05537)
*Baichuan Zhao,Qi Sun,Nan Li,Chih-Lin I*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Lyapunov的完成感知MAC调度器，通过虚拟债务队列与漂移惩罚指标，显著提升XR PDU集在时限内的交付可靠性及低尾性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时扩展现实（XR）服务对吞吐量、时延和可靠性要求极为严格；现有PDU集感知MAC调度器仅按紧迫性或进度排序，未显式考虑PDU集是否能在时限内完成，导致交付可靠性不足。

Method: 基于Lyapunov优化框架，联合捕获每个队首PDU集的完成依赖、截止期限紧迫性和完成可行性；通过虚拟债务队列建模长期QoS需求，设计漂移惩罚调度指标，根据资源对各队首PDU集及时完成概率的边际贡献进行分配。

Result: 系统级仿真表明，所提调度器在资源竞争场景下显著提高PDU集交付可靠性与低尾性能，并在异构流量需求和网络条件下保持稳健表现。

Conclusion: 该调度器有效平衡长期可靠性与即时完成机会，为实时XR服务提供更可靠的MAC层调度，满足严格QoS要求。

Abstract: Real-time extended reality (XR) services impose stringent throughput, latency, and reliability requirements. An XR media unit is commonly segmented into a protocol data unit (PDU) set that is useful only when all required PDUs are delivered within its delay budget. Existing PDU set-aware medium access control (MAC) schedulers generally prioritize transmissions according to urgency or progress, without explicitly accounting for whether a PDU set can still be completed on time. In this paper, we propose a Lyapunov-based completion-aware MAC scheduler that jointly captures the set-level completion dependency, deadline urgency, and completion feasibility of each head-of-line PDU set. Long-term quality-of-service (QoS) requirements are represented through virtual debt queues, and resources are allocated according to their marginal contribution to the timely-completion probability of each head-of-line PDU set. These components are integrated into a drift-plus-penalty scheduling metric that balances long-term reliability requirements against current completion opportunities. System-level simulations demonstrate that the proposed scheduler improves PDU set delivery reliability and lower-tail performance under resource contention, while providing robust performance across heterogeneous traffic demands and network conditions.

</details>


### [310] [Radio-FM: A Foundation Model for Radio Signal Representation Learning and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05793)
*Jinchao Zhou,Wupeng Xie,Zhuangzhi Chen,Yao Lu,Qi Xuan,Yun Lin,Guan Gui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Radio-FM是一个面向无线电射频(RF)领域的可扩展基础模型家族，采用双通道处理机制专门优化I/Q信号独立性，同时通过轻量级注意力机制捕获跨通道交互。论文提出了token预算动态批处理策略配合通道独立掩码重建，在15个跨调制、雷达、通信领域的数据集上进行预训练，并在15个下游基准测试中评估。实验结果表明Radio-FM在13/15个基准测试中达到最先进性能，展现出优越的少样本迁移能力，特别是在数据稀缺场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基础模型应用于射频领域面临独特挑战：原始I/Q信号具有固有的物理复杂性，频谱数据存在极端异构性。现有架构难以有效处理这些特性，需要专门设计的基础模型来解决RF信号表示学习的核心难题。

Method: 1. **双通道处理架构**：专门优化I/Q信号独立处理，同时通过轻量级注意力机制捕获跨通道交互关系
2. **Token预算动态批处理策略**：解决异构多源语料库中序列长度高度可变的预训练缩放问题
3. **通道独立掩码重建**：配合动态批处理策略实现高效预训练
4. **大规模预训练**：在涵盖调制、雷达和通信领域的15个多样化数据集上进行预训练

Result: 1. **性能表现**：在15个下游基准测试中，13个达到最先进(SOTA)性能
2. **跨任务改进**：在调制识别、雷达识别、发射机识别、无线技术识别和无线干扰识别任务中持续改进
3. **少样本迁移能力**：在数据稀缺场景下显著优于现有基线方法
4. **泛化能力验证**：在多个射频信号理解任务中展现出通用性

Conclusion: Radio-FM作为射频信号理解的通用骨干模型具有巨大潜力。其双通道处理机制和动态批处理策略有效解决了RF领域的独特挑战，特别是在数据稀缺场景下的优越迁移性能验证了其作为射频信号理解通用基础模型的实用价值。该工作为RF领域的基础模型发展提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Applying foundation models to the radio frequency (RF) domain presents unique challenges due to the intrinsic physical complexity of raw I/Q signals and the extreme heterogeneity of spectral data. In this paper, we present Radio-FM, a scalable family of foundation models designed for universal radio signal representation learning. Unlike standard architectures, Radio-FM employs dual-channel processing specifically optimized for I/Q independence while capturing cross-channel interactions through a lightweight attention mechanism. To scale pretraining across heterogeneous multi-source corpora with highly variable sequence lengths, we propose a token-budgeted dynamic batching strategy coupled with channel-independent masked reconstruction. We pretrain Radio-FM on a diverse collection of 15 datasets spanning modulation, radar, and communication domains, and rigorously evaluate it on 15 downstream benchmarks. Experimental results show Radio-FM achieves state-of-the-art performance on 13 of 15 benchmarks, consistently improving across modulation, radar, emitter identification, wireless technology recognition, and wireless interference identification. Notably, it exhibits superior few-shot transferability, significantly outperforming existing baselines in data-scarce regimes, validating its potential as a general-purpose backbone for radio signal understanding.

</details>


### [311] [Exact DC Representation of Multi-Tier Offloading Product in SAGINs via Quantifier Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05978)
*Minh-Tuong Nguyen,Vo Phi Son,Dinh Thai Hoang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对空间-空中-地面集成网络(SAGIN)中任务卸载导致的非凸单项式/多项式优化问题，提出一种基于差分凸(DC)表示与凸凹程序(CCP)的求解方法，该方法在实数域上经量词消去严格验证，其SOCP子问题可兼容嵌入式代码生成器(如CVXPYgen)，并与全局求解器BARON对比验证了接近全局最优的解，同时将平均求解时间从SGP的0.1012秒降至0.0113秒，实现8.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务卸载在SAGIN中产生非凸优化问题，通常包含三次耦合的单项式或多项式项；现有序贯几何规划(SGP)虽可近似处理，但其指数锥表示超出嵌入式代码生成器(如CVXPYgen)所支持的二阶锥规划(SOCP)范畴，限制了实际部署可行性，因此需要一种既能保持求解精度又可转化为SOCP子问题的新方法。

Method: 首先通过量词消去对原非凸问题进行精确的差分凸(DC)表示；随后采用凸凹程序(CCP)迭代求解，每个CCP子问题被转化为SOCP形式，从而避免指数锥约束，为未来嵌入式代码生成扫清结构障碍；最后与全局求解器BARON进行对比验证。

Result: 实验表明，SGP与CCP均能获得接近全局最优的解（以BARON为基准）；CCP的平均求解时间从SGP的0.1012秒大幅降至0.0113秒，实现8.9倍加速，同时其SOCP子问题结构便于后续嵌入式部署。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DC+CCP方法在求解SAGIN任务卸载非凸优化问题时兼具高效性与可嵌入性，不仅显著提升了求解速度，还通过SOCP子问题结构为未来在嵌入式系统上的代码生成与部署奠定了基础，是兼顾性能与实践可行性的有效方案。

Abstract: Task offloading in space--air--ground integrated networks (SAGIN) yields non-convex signomial or polynomial programs with cubic couplings. Sequential geometric programming (SGP) approximates them via exponential cone representations, which exceeds the second-order cone programming (SOCP) ceiling of embedded code generators such as CVXPYgen. We derive a difference-of-convex (DC) representation exactly certified over the reals by quantifier elimination and apply the convex--concave procedure (CCP), whose SOCP subproblems remove this structural obstacle to future embedded code generation. Comparisons with the BARON global solver show that SGP and CCP both attain near-global solutions. CCP further reduces the average solution time from SGP's $0.1012$~s to $0.0113$~s, an $8.9$-fold speedup.

</details>


### [312] [Tracking performance of RLS algorithms in WSSUS channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06036)
*Yuriy Zakharov,Lu Shen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析RLS自适应算法在WSSUS时变信道中跟踪性能的理论方法，通过PSD的谱矩推导了均方偏差（MSD）的一般公式，并验证了分析结果与仿真的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应算法广泛应用于线性时变系统（如通信信道）的估计，其跟踪性能受噪声水平、时变特性及算法参数影响。优化参数并预测算法性能是重要任务，需要理论分析工具来指导算法设计和参数选择。

Method: 将信道时变特性建模为WSSUS随机过程，以其功率谱密度（PSD）表征；针对指数RLS和滑动窗口RLS（SRLS）算法，推导MSD的一般公式（用PSD的谱矩表示）；将结果推广至带延迟的RLS算法及采用Legendre多项式近似的SRLS-L算法。

Result: 获得了MSD的解析表达式，针对均匀、Jakes和自回归PSD进行了具体化；数值示例显示分析MSD与仿真结果吻合良好，验证了理论的正确性。

Conclusion: 该理论方法能有效预测RLS类算法在时变信道中的跟踪性能，为算法参数优化提供指导，分析结果得到仿真验证，具有良好的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Adaptive algorithms are widely used for estimation of linear time-varying systems, such as communication channels. Their tracking performance depends on the level of noise, characteristics of time variations, and the algorithm parameters. Optimizing these parameters and predicting the algorithm performance is an important task. In this paper, we present an approach for analysing the tracking performance of recursive least squares (RLS) adaptive algorithms in channels with time variations described as wide-sense stationary uncorrelated scattering (WSSUS) random processes characterised by a power spectral density (PSD). We focus on exponential RLS and sliding-window RLS (SRLS) algorithms, for which general formulas for the mean square deviation (MSD) as a measure of the tracking performance are obtained in terms of spectral moments of the PSD. As examples, they are specified for random processes with uniform, Jakes' and autoregressive PSDs. These results are further generalized to RLS algorithms with delays, e.g. processing delays or delays introduced in non-causal adaptive RLS algorithms, and to the SRLS-L algorithm with approximation of channel time variations using Legendre polynomials. Numerical examples show good match between the analytical MSD and simulation results.

</details>


### [313] [Numerical Model of a Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output Distributed Acoustic Sensing System with Joint Phase and Birefringence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06198)
*Diane Prato,Mehran Mokhtari Sheramin,Renaud Gabet,Elie Awwad*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种考虑光纤双折射动态扰动和背散射信号共同光学相位的多输入多输出分布式声学传感（MIMO‑DAS）数值模型。采用偏振复用恒定功率编码序列，使DAS与WDM数据在同一光纤中共存。通过数值仿真和实验，实现静态双折射幅度估计，以及动态纵向应变和各向异性横向应变下的共同相位与双折射幅度联合估计，从而实现事件判别和对横向扰动的更高灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高分布式声学传感系统在复杂扰动环境下的探测能力，尤其是区分轴对称事件与各向异性事件，并实现DAS与WDM传输在同一光纤中共存，需要建立考虑光纤双折射动态变化和共同光学相位的MIMO‑DAS模型。

Method: 构建包含光纤双折射动态扰动和背散射共同光学相位的MIMO‑DAS数值模型，采用偏振复用恒定功率编码序列对光纤进行探测；通过数值仿真和实验室实验验证模型性能。

Result: 在静态条件下可估计有效双折射幅度；在动态纵向应变和各向异性横向应变下，可实现共同相位与双折射幅度的联合估计。该能力使系统能够判别不同事件类型，并对横向扰动具有更高灵敏度。

Conclusion: 所提出的MIMO‑DAS数值模型能够有效区分轴对称与各向异性扰动，提升系统对横向扰动的灵敏度，并为分布式声学传感与WDM数据在同一光纤中共存提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce and experimentally validate a numerical model for a Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output Distributed Acoustic Sensing (MIMO-DAS) system that accounts for dynamic perturbations of fiber birefringence and of the common optical phase of the backscattered signal (or polarization-averaged phase, shared by both polarization tributaries). The MIMO-DAS system probes the fiber using polarization-multiplexed constant-power coded sequences that are suited for coexistence of DAS with WDM data transmission over the same fiber. We study the effect of both axisymmetric and anisotropic events on the two quantities. We demonstrate, through numerical simulations and lab experiments, the estimation of effective birefringence magnitude in static conditions, and the joint estimation of common phase and effective birefringence magnitude in the case of dynamic longitudinal strain and anisotropic transverse strain. This allows for event discrimination and increased sensitivity to disturbances that act transversely on the fiber, since polarization will be responsive to perturbations that break cylindrical symmetry, while the phase will strongly respond to longitudinal strain.

</details>


### [314] [Neural CRC Prediction for 5G NR URLLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06230)
*Prashanth Murthy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于5G NR物理上行共享信道(PUSCH)的神经循环冗余校验(CRC)预测器，通过轻量级CNN与固定前端结合，直接从接收信号提取多尺度时频能量特征，绕过传统均衡和解码链，实现超低可靠低延迟通信(URLLC)的早期链路自适应决策。实验表明混合散射预测器显著优于纯CNN基线，MCS条件化进一步提升了不同信道条件下的可靠性，且支持GPU加速实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G NR中的URLLC应用需要超低延迟和高可靠性，传统的接收机流程(均衡、解码)耗时较长，难以满足早期链路自适应决策的需求。因此，需要一种能够直接从接收信号快速预测CRC结果的方法，以支持实时链路自适应。

Method: 设计了一种结合轻量级卷积神经网络(CNN)和固定前端的神经CRC预测器。固定前端有两种实现方式：基于小波散射的前端(使用固定Gabor滤波器)和基于FFT的散射前端(在频域应用带通掩码，几何尺度间隔)。前端从接收信号和最小二乘信道估计中提取多尺度时频能量特征。预测器直接估计解码后的CRC结果，绕过传统均衡和解码链。此外，引入调制编码方案(MCS)索引作为辅助条件输入，以适应工作码率调整决策边界。实验使用多MCS 5G NR PUSCH数据集进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，混合散射预测器显著优于纯CNN基线。MCS条件化进一步提高了在不同信道条件下的可靠性。两种前端均兼容GPU加速推理，足够轻量以部署在实时基带流水线中。

Conclusion: 提出的神经CRC预测器能够实现5G NR PUSCH的早期链路自适应决策，满足URLLC的延迟和可靠性要求。混合散射前端与CNN的结合优于纯CNN基线，MCS条件化增强了适应性。方法支持实时GPU加速部署，并通过证据深度学习扩展量化认知不确定性。该工作为5G物理层机器学习接收机设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We propose a neural cyclic redundancy check (CRC) predictor for the 5G New Radio (5G NR) physical uplink shared channel (PUSCH) that enables early link-adaptation decisions for Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communications (URLLC). The predictor combines a lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) with a fixed front-end that extracts multi-scale time-frequency energy features from the received signal and least-squares channel estimates. We investigate two complementary front-end realizations - a wavelet scattering front-end built from fixed Gabor filters, and an FFT-based scattering front-end that applies bandpass masks in the frequency domain with geometric scale spacing. Drawing on neural-receiver design principles, the predictor estimates the post-decoding CRC outcome directly from the received resource grid, bypassing the conventional equalization and decoding chain. We further introduce the modulation and coding scheme (MCS) index as an auxiliary conditioning input that adapts the decision boundary to the operating code rate. Experiments on a multi-MCS 5G NR PUSCH dataset show that the hybrid scattering predictors substantially outperform a pure CNN baseline, with MCS conditioning further improving reliability across varying channel conditions. Both front-ends are compatible with GPU-accelerated inference and are lightweight enough to be deployed within a real-time baseband pipeline. We further demonstrate an evidential deep learning extension that quantifies epistemic uncertainty in a single forward pass using a conservative decision rule.

</details>


### [315] [Joint Access Point Selection and Precoder Design under Statistical CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06251)
*Tim N. Faisst,Franz Weißer,Wolfgang Utschick*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究多接入点多用户系统中基于统计信道状态信息的联合接入点选择和预编码优化问题，提出迭代交替优化算法和图神经网络框架两种方法，实验表明GNN方法在性能上优于迭代算法且具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多接入点多用户系统中，联合优化接入点选择和预编码策略是实现和速率最大化的关键挑战。特别是在仅具有统计信道状态信息（CSI）的情况下，如何高效地完成这一优化问题具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Method: 本文提出两种方法：（1）迭代交替优化算法，通过随机WMMSE（SWMMSE）算法更新预编码向量，通过投影梯度下降步骤更新分配变量；（2）基于图神经网络的框架，采用注意力机制的Edge-GNN架构，扩展至多接入点场景，从统计CSI中联合学习分配变量和预编码向量，在推理阶段通过单次前向传播解决问题。

Result: 实验结果显示，GNN方法在测试的信噪比（SNR）范围内均优于迭代算法，并且能够泛化至不同数量的用户场景，保持相当的性能水平。两种方法均与多种基线技术进行了比较。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的框架在联合接入点选择和预编码优化问题中展现出优越的性能和泛化能力，为统计CSI条件下的多接入点多用户系统优化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This work addresses joint access point (AP) selection and precoding for sum-rate maximization under statistical channel state information (CSI) in multi-AP multi-user systems. To this end, we propose two approaches. The first method is an iterative alternating optimization algorithm that updates the precoding vectors via the stochastic WMMSE (SWMMSE) algorithm and the assignment variables via a projected gradient descent step. The second method is a graph neural network (GNN)-based framework that solves the same problem in a single forward pass during inference. Building on an attention-based Edge-GNN architecture, we extend it to a multi-AP scenario, enabling the joint learning of assignment variables and precoding vectors from statistical CSI alone. Results show that the GNN outperforms the iterative algorithm across the tested signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range and generalizes to varying numbers of users with comparable performance. Both approaches are also compared to various baseline techniques.

</details>


### [316] [An Analysis of the Accuracy of the Added Length De-Embedding Methods for Coaxial to Waveguide Adapters in the X-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06313)
*Eric Bauman,James DePoy,Sterling Light,Arya Menon,Marco Iskander*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究评估了X波段同轴-波导转换器去嵌入中"加长度法"的准确性，与完整波导校准方法对比，发现该方法仅适用于传输测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏专用波导校准套件或转换器完整S参数表征的情况下，研究数学校正技术的可行性和准确性，平衡成本与测量精度之间的关系。

Method: 在Keysight和Rohde & Schwarz矢量网络分析仪上，对比"加长度法"（端口延伸/通道偏移）与完整波导校准方法在X波段同轴-波导转换器去嵌入中的表现。

Result: 分析结果表明，使用同轴-波导转换器时，加长度法仅在传输测量中产生准确结果。

Conclusion: 加长度法是一种经济可行的数学校正技术，适用于传输测量场景，但在其他测量模式下存在局限性，不建议在缺少专用校准设备的条件下用于全面表征。

Abstract: This paper assesses the accuracy of the "added length method" (also known as port extension or channel offset) for de-embedding coaxial-to-waveguide transitions in the X-band, comparing it to full waveguide calibration on both Keysight and Rohde & Schwarz VNAs. The study examines the trade-offs between cost and measurement accuracy, highlighting the feasibility of using these mathematical correction techniques in the absence of dedicated waveguide calibration kits or full S-parameter characterizations of the transitions. The analysis concluded that when using coaxial-to-waveguide transitions the added length method produces accurate results for transmission measurements only.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Multi-Agent Transformer for Queue-Level XR Traffic Scheduling in TSN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05340)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多智能体强化学习的XR流量调度方法，采用多智能体Transformer建模队列间依赖关系，实现异构XR应用间的隐式协调，显著降低延迟和失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSN和MEC为实现XR等时间敏感应用的超低延迟通信具有潜力，但XR的广泛部署带来了以下挑战：(1)MEC环境中共址服务导致共享网络资源竞争；(2)XR流量类型具有不同的时间要求和关键性，增加了环境复杂性和动态性；(3)现有RL方法依赖集中式或高层多智能体设计，主要针对周期性可预测的工业流量，难以应用于XR工作负载；(4)现有方法存在两个局限：细粒度控制能力有限，难以捕获队列间依赖关系；对高度动态异构XR流量的适应性差。

Method: 提出一种面向队列级XR流量调度的多智能体强化学习方法。采用多智能体Transformer(MAT)通过注意力机制建模智能体观察和行动间的队列间依赖关系，实现异构共址XR应用间的隐式协调。

Result: 仿真结果显示所提方法优于基线方法，实现了最高71.42%的延迟降低和最高83.2%的失败率降低，同时在所有队列中持续保持高可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于多智能体Transformer的队列级调度方法有效解决了现有方法在XR流量调度中的局限，在TSN/MEC环境下实现了显著的性能提升，能够适应高度动态异构的XR流量特性。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) and Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) hold strong potential for enabling ultra-reliable low-latency communication for time-sensitive applications, such as eXtended Reality (XR). However, the widespread adoption of XR introduces significant challenges due to co-located services in MEC environments, leading to contention for shared network resources. Moreover, XR traffic types have distinct characteristics and criticality in terms of timing requirements, further increasing the complexity and dynamics of such environments. Although reinforcement learning has shown promise for TSN scheduling optimization in dynamic network scenarios, existing approaches rely on centralized or high-level multi-agent designs and are typically tailored to periodic and predictable industrial traffic, limiting their applicability to XR workloads. As a result, these approaches suffer from (i) limited ability to capture inter-queue dependencies due to coarse-grained control, and (ii) poor adaptability to highly dynamic and heterogeneous XR traffic. To address these gaps, we propose a multi-agent reinforcement learning approach for queue-level XR traffic scheduling. We adopt the multi-agent transformer (MAT) to model inter-queue dependencies via attention over agents' observations and actions, enabling implicit coordination across heterogeneous co-located XR applications. Our simulation results show that the proposed method outperforms baselines, achieving up to 71.42% latency reduction and up to 83.2% reduction in failure rate, while consistently achieving high reliability across all queues.

</details>


### [318] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Online Traffic Scheduling in Time-Sensitive Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05346)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多智能体强化学习的TSN调度框架，用于解决移动边缘计算中XR应用的动态流量调度问题，相比传统方法显著降低帧等待时间和最坏情况延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSN正越来越多地与MEC集成以支持严格延迟要求的XR应用，但现有TSN调度方案主要依赖静态优化技术或基于固定流量模式的集中式学习模型，在动态环境中效果有限。实际MEC环境中存在多个共存且特征随时间演变的XR流量流，产生复杂的队列间依赖关系，当前调度器无法有效捕获这些关系，因此需要自适应、去中心化的调度机制。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架用于TSN调度，将每个TSN队列建模为自治智能体。采用异构智能体近端策略优化(HAPPO)算法，显式建模智能体间依赖关系并联合优化跨队列的服务交付。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法将平均帧等待时间减少多达26.8%，最坏情况延迟减少约16.8%。

Conclusion: 所提MARL方法在动态XR驱动的MEC场景中表现出显著有效性，克服了现有静态/集中式调度方法的局限性，能够为多队列协同优化提供自适应、去中心化的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-sensitive networking (TSN) is increasingly integrated into mobile edge computing (MEC) to support applications with stringent latency requirements, such as extended reality (XR). However, existing TSN scheduling solutions predominantly rely on static optimization techniques or centralized learning models that are based on fixed traffic patterns, limiting their effectiveness in dynamic environments. In practice, MEC environments often host multiple co-located XR traffic flows whose characteristics evolve over time, creating complex inter-queue dependencies that current schedulers fail to capture. Addressing these challenges requires adaptive, decentralized scheduling mechanisms capable of coordinating multiple TSN queues under varying traffic conditions. To this end, this paper proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for TSN scheduling, where each TSN queue is modeled as an autonomous agent. The Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) algorithm is employed to explicitly model inter-agent dependencies and jointly optimize service delivery across queues. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces average frame waiting times by up to 26.8% and worst-case delays by approximately 16.8%, highlighting its effectiveness in dynamic XR-driven MEC scenarios.

</details>


### [319] [DTMC-Based Analysis and Scheduling for Periodic Flows with Proactive HARQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05639)
*Haozhe Yi,Junyi Liu,Maolin Yang,Haochun Liang,Bo Liu,Feng Hong,Chaowei Liu,Hongbiao Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）的分析框架，结合双阶段遗传算法，用于解决带有主动HARQ的周期性URLLC流量调度问题，在保证异构可靠性和延迟约束的同时提高可调度性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URLLC要求对异构周期性流量提供严格的可靠性和延迟保证。主动HARQ通过提前终止机制提高了资源效率，但时隙级时序效应（尤其是延迟反馈）使得可调度性分析变得复杂。

Method: 构建了一个DTMC模型，通过扩展状态空间来捕获HARQ往返时间及跨时隙时序效应。该框架确定满足异构约束所需的传输机会，并采用双阶段遗传算法实现基于偏移量的调度。

Result: 工业URLLC流量仿真表明，该方法在可接受计算开销下，相比反应式HARQ、K重复和非保证主动HARQ，实现了更高的可调度性。

Conclusion: 所提出的DTMC框架结合主动HARQ和遗传算法调度，能有效提升工业URLLC场景下的系统可调度性，同时保持计算复杂度可控，为5G/6G URLLC设计提供了实用的分析工具和解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) requires strict reliability and latency guarantees for heterogeneous periodic traffic. Proactive HARQ improves resource efficiency through early termination, but slot-level timing effects, particularly delayed feedback, complicate schedulability analysis.
  This paper presents a discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC)-based framework for periodic flows with proactive HARQ. By expanding the state space, the model captures HARQ round-trip time and other cross-slot timing effects. The framework determines the transmission opportunities required to satisfy heterogeneous reliability and latency constraints and supports offset-based scheduling through a two-stage genetic algorithm.
  Simulations with industrial URLLC traffic show that the proposed method achieves higher schedulability than reactive HARQ, K-Repetition, and non-guaranteed proactive HARQ, with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [320] [Closed-Loop Decision-Focused Learning for User-Aware Cloud Orchestration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05735)
*Dongbin Jiao,Xubo Zhang,Huakang Lin,Ke Shang,Shi Yan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭环决策聚焦学习框架（CL-DFL），通过整合时空预测器和零阶优化算法，实现云资源调度中违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云工作负载具有时变性，导致低峰期资源利用不足和高峰期资源争用。现有的预测-优化（PTO）框架存在两阶段解耦问题，难以在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间取得平衡。

Method: 将异构作业调度建模为不确定约束下的多目标组合优化问题（MOCOP），提出CL-DFL框架：1）基于MTGNN的时空预测器进行资源感知；2）基于TPE的零阶DFL机制进行决策优化；3）建立端到端反馈通路；4）开发GNeuro-PLS策略，将组相对策略优化（GRPO）融入协同局部搜索，提升异构工作负载下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，CL-DFL在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间实现了更优的权衡。在常规工作负载下有效控制过载风险，在高饱和场景下保持韧性，优于现有先进基线方法。

Conclusion: CL-DFL通过端到端闭环架构有效解决了传统PTO框架的两阶段解耦问题，为云编排提供了更优的决策机制，在多种负载场景下均展现出卓越性能。

Abstract: Time-varying cloud workloads often cause resource under-utilization during off-peak periods and resource contention during peak periods. Existing prediction-then-optimization (PTO) frameworks suffer from two-stage decoupling, hindering the balance among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. We formulate heterogeneous job scheduling as a multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem (MOCOP) under uncertain constraints and propose a closed-loop decision-focused learning (CL-DFL) framework for cloud orchestration. CL-DFL integrates a Multivariate Time-series Graph Neural Network (MTGNN)-based spatio-temporal predictor with a zeroth-order decision-focused learning (DFL) mechanism based on the tree-structured Parzen estimator (TPE). This integration establishes an end-to-end (E2E) feedback pathway between resource perception and scheduling decisions. Furthermore, we develop the GNeuro-PLS strategy by incorporating group relative policy optimization (GRPO) into cooperative local search to improve robustness under heterogeneous workloads. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that CL-DFL achieves superior trade-offs among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. It effectively controls overload risks under regular workloads and maintains resilience under highly saturated scenarios compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [321] [5G ISAC-Based UAV Detection and 3-D Tracking Using Uplink Sounding Reference Signals on an End-to-End O-RAN Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05826)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan,Shiva R. Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个基于O-RAN架构的5G ISAC（通感一体）端到端仿真测试床，利用现有的NR上行探测参考信号实现低空无人机探测和三维跟踪，无需修改标准或专用波形，同时在10 Mbps通信负载下保持探测覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通感一体（ISAC）旨在让蜂窝基础设施同时服务于通信用户和感知任务，共享同一波形。低空无人机检测与三维跟踪是当前重要应用场景，但需要在不引入专用感知波形的情况下实现，以避免改变现有5G标准并保证通信性能。

Method: 基于开源组件（OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC、Sionna RT）构建O-RAN仿真测试床，将NR上行探测参考信号复用为被动雷达波形；在gNB内部实现物理层感知模块，产生的检测数据通过自定义E2服务模型送至扩展卡尔曼滤波跟踪xApp。针对单双基地配置下高度不可观测问题，采用两种方法：平面接收阵列提供垂直孔径，以及第二个发射机提供距离多样性；结合实时实验与离线射线追踪进行验证。

Result: 实时结果与离线射线追踪研究相互印证，估计器从故意错误初始高度收敛至1.8 m RMSE的稳态滤波器，证明高度可观性已在信号处理链路和端到端栈中得到建立。在并发10 Mbps上行通信负载下，探测覆盖得以保持。

Conclusion: 该O-RAN基ISAC测试床成功实现了基于现有NR上行信号的无人机探测与三维跟踪，无需专用感知波形，验证了高度可观性，并保持了通信性能，为5G通感一体技术的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) lets cellular infrastructure serve communication users and sense on the same waveform. We present an end-to-end O-RAN simulation testbed for 5G ISAC targeting low-altitude UAV detection and 3-D tracking, built from open-source components: OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT, in which the NR Uplink Sounding Reference Signal is repurposed as a passive radar waveform: a PHY-layer sensing stage inside the gNB produces detections that reach an Extended Kalman Filter tracking xApp over a custom E2 service model, with no change to the NR standard and no dedicated sensing waveform. A single bistatic pair leaves elevation unobservable, so the tracker needs a height prior; we remove it two independent ways and measure both - a planar receive array supplying a vertical aperture, and a second transmitter supplying range diversity. Both live results corroborate an offline ray-traced study of the same estimator, which converges from a deliberately wrong initial altitude to 1.8 m RMSE at a consistent filter, so altitude observability is established both in the signal-processing chain and end to end through the live stack. Detection coverage is preserved under a concurrent 10 Mbps uplink communications load.

</details>


### [322] [BALANCE: Hybrid Autoregressive-Speculative LLM Inference in Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05926)
*Guanqiao Qu,Shuo Chen,Qian Chen,Kin K. Leung,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Edge inference is a promising paradigm to provide large language model (LLM) inference services in next-generation mobile networks. LLM inference mainly relies on two approaches: Autoregressive decoding (AD) generates output tokens sequentially, resulting in long latency; Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates inference by using a small language model (SLM) to generate multiple draft tokens for LLM verification, but incurs extra memory costs. Due to this latency-memory tradeoff, neither approach alone can efficiently serve users with heterogeneous demands under limited edge computing resources. To address this challenge, we propose a hybrid autoregressive-speculative inference (BALANCE) framework for edge LLM inference. In BALANCE, an edge server hosts both an SLM and an LLM, assigns each user to AD or SD, and performs the two modes simultaneously. To maximize the number of served users, we formulate a task throughput maximization problem to jointly determine user scheduling and computing resource allocation between AD and SD under user latency requirements and server memory constraints. Since the problem is NP-hard, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm that transforms the original problem into two sub-problems and obtains a sub-optimal solution with a constant approximation guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that BALANCE consistently outperforms conventional AD and SD and significantly improves task throughput.

</details>


### [323] [ML-for-ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06046)
*Yutong Zhao,Noga H. Rotman,Gianni Antichi,Ran Ben Basat*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对云集群中网络与机器学习训练参数分别优化导致的效率损失问题，提出了一种跨层联合优化方法（ML-for-ML），在初步原型中实现了高达42%的收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练工作量快速增长，使得时间、能源和基础设施成本日益重要。在共享云集群中，训练作业与并发作业竞争网络资源，但网络机制和ML训练选择通常被分别优化，导致端到端性能未达最优。

Method: 提出ML-for-ML跨层视角，在网络侧和ML侧参数下，基于共享的“达到目标损失时间”目标进行联合优化。

Result: 初步原型表明，通过联合优化ML和网络参数，达到目标损失的速度提升高达42%。

Conclusion: 网络与ML训练的分离优化存在效率损失，跨层联合优化可显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: AI training workloads are growing rapidly, making their time, energy, and infrastructure costs increasingly important. In shared cloud clusters, training and fine-tuning jobs compete with co-running workloads for network resources, while network mechanisms and ML training choices are typically optimized separately: networking controls how bytes move, whereas ML systems control when and how much communication occurs. We argue that this separation leaves end-to-end performance on the table.
  We present ML-for-ML, a cross-layer perspective in which network-side and ML-side knobs are selected jointly under a shared time-to-target-loss objective. Our preliminary prototype shows that by co-optimizing the ML and network parameters, we reach the target loss up to 42% faster.

</details>


### [324] [MARS: Multipath Adaptive Reliable Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06101)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS，一种接收端驱动、转发器辅助的Internet/WAN多路径传输方案。通过层同步的覆盖路径发现和耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制，MARS能够安全扩展可用转发机会并接近瓶颈快速响应。作为UDP覆盖层在客户端、服务器、中继或CDN节点上增量部署。通过大规模仿真和Mininet仿真评估，结果显示MARS在仅端点部署时与现有端到端多路径基线相当；在合作转发器部署下，相比ECMP基线最大p95流完成时间减少最多81.5%；即使与路径扩展的端到端基线相比，MARS在最大p95 FCT、丢包鲁棒性和瞬态链路故障恢复方面表现更优。证明ICN式接收端驱动转发可作为WAN多路径的部署覆盖传输基质，无需修改IP路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路径传输对于跨异构路径传输大量数据的Internet/WAN服务（如地理分布式分析、内容分发、云服务管道）越来越重要。现有解决方案存在实际权衡：端到端传输如MPTCP和MPQUIC可部署但受限于端点可见路径和延迟拥塞反馈；而路由或转发器辅助方案往往需要基础设施支持或缺乏转发选择的安全协调。需要一种能安全扩展可用转发机会并接近瓶颈响应的方案，且可增量部署。

Method: MARS是一种接收端驱动、转发器辅助的多路径传输。结合层同步覆盖路径发现与耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制，实现安全扩展可用转发机会和近瓶颈反应。作为UDP覆盖层在客户端、服务器、中继或类似CDN节点上增量部署。通过大规模仿真和Mininet仿真，在不同部署规模、丢包率和故障场景下评估。

Result: MARS提供依赖部署的收益：仅端点部署时，与端到端多路径基线保持竞争力；合作覆盖转发器部署下，展示更丰富的可用路径多样性，相比ECMP有限基线最大p95流完成时间减少最多81.5%。即使与路径扩展的端到端基线相比，MARS实现更低的最差情况p95 FCT和更强丢包鲁棒性，同时从瞬态链路故障中快速恢复。

Conclusion: ICN式接收端驱动转发可作为WAN多路径的部署覆盖传输基质，在不修改IP路由的情况下提供超越纯端到端设计的益处。

Abstract: Multipath transport is increasingly important for Internet/WAN services that move large data volumes across heterogeneous paths, including geo-distributed analytics, content distribution, and cloud-service pipelines. Existing solutions, however, face a practical trade-off: end-to-end transports such as MPTCP and MPQUIC are deployable but limited by endpoint-visible paths and delayed congestion feedback, while routing-or forwarder-assisted approaches often require infrastructure support or lack safe coordination across forwarding choices. This paper presents MARS, a receiver-driven, forwarder-assisted multipath transport for Internet/WAN environments. MARS combines tier-synchronized overlay path discovery with coupled consumer/forwarder congestion control, enabling it to safely expand usable forwarding opportunities and react near bottlenecks. It runs as an incrementally deployable UDP overlay at clients, servers, relays, or CDN-like nodes. We implement MARS in simulation and as a prototype, and evaluate it through large-scale simulation and Mininet emulation under different deployment scales, loss rates, and failure scenarios. The results show that MARS provides deployment-dependent benefits: with endpoint-only deployment, it remains competitive with end-to-end multipath baselines; with cooperating overlay forwarders, it exposes richer usable path diversity and reduces max p95 flow completion time by up to 81.5\% over ECMP-limited baselines. Even against path-expanded end-to-end baselines given the same path set, MARS achieves lower worst-case p95 FCT and stronger robustness under packet loss, while also recovering quickly from transient link failures. These results demonstrate that ICN-style receiver-driven forwarding can serve as a deployable overlay transport substrate for WAN multipath, providing benefits beyond purely end-to-end designs without requiring changes to IP routing.

</details>


### [325] [MultiMoQ: Multi-Access Media-Over-QUIC for Robust Immersive Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06102)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Ruonan Chai,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于MoQ的多访问瓦片流媒体框架MultiMoQ，通过多访问控制实现鲁棒的高比特率沉浸式视频传输，相比DASH和标准MoQ提高了吞吐量并降低了延迟，实现了更流畅的播放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时沉浸式视频（360度视频）在虚拟活动、体育转播和远程教育中应用日益广泛，但现有方法难以支持大量并发用户的高比特率流媒体传输，存在粗粒度交付限制响应能力、缺乏并发瓦片流协调等问题。MoQ虽然是大规模式媒体传输的有前景方案，但在带宽受限条件下缺乏鲁棒性，经常导致播放卡顿。

Method: 提出MultiMoQ，一个基于MoQ的多访问瓦片流媒体框架，重新设计交付机制以支持跨多访问路径的鲁棒高比特率流媒体传输，同时支持灵活的瓦片调度和无缝访问切换。实现完整原型并在异构真实网络条件下进行网络模拟评估，与DASH和标准MoQ进行比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比DASH，MultiMoQ提高了增强瓦片和基础视频的吞吐量，降低了增强瓦片的尾部端到端延迟，同时保持音频连续性，避免了标准MoQ的持续卡顿。传输层改进转化为更平滑的视口播放，消融实验进一步确认了多访问控制对播放连续性的贡献。

Conclusion: MultiMoQ通过多访问控制有效解决了高比特率沉浸式流媒体传输的鲁棒性挑战，相比现有解决方案在吞吐量、延迟和播放连续性方面均有显著改善。

Abstract: Live immersive video streaming, particularly 360-degree video, is increasingly adopted in applications such as virtual events, sports broadcasting, and remote education. Existing approaches struggle to support high-bitrate immersive streaming for large numbers of concurrent users, with coarse-grained delivery limiting responsiveness and insufficient support for coordinating concurrent tile streams. Media over QUIC (MoQ) has recently emerged as a promising solution for large-scale media delivery, yet it lacks robustness under bandwidth-constrained conditions, often resulting in playback stalls. To address these challenges, we present MultiMoQ, a multi-access tile streaming framework built on MoQ that redesigns its delivery mechanism for robust high-bitrate streaming across multiple access paths while supporting flexible tile scheduling and seamless access switching. We implement a fully functional prototype of MultiMoQ and evaluate it in network emulation under heterogeneous real-world network conditions, comparing against Dynamic Adaptive Streaming over HTTP (DASH) and standard MoQ. Results show that MultiMoQ increases goodput for enhancement tiles and base video and reduces enhancement-tile tail end-to-end latency relative to DASH, while preserving audio continuity and avoiding the persistent stalls of standard MoQ. These transport gains translate into smoother viewport playback, and the ablation results further confirm the contribution of multi-access control to playback continuity.

</details>


### [326] [From Passive Mirrors to Active Agents: Holonic Digital Twins for Physical AI over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06227)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Omar Hashash,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HDT-Nets（全息数字孪生网络）框架，通过分层数字孪生和多种理论工具（因果马尔可夫毯、主动推理、范畴论、整合信息论）实现物理AI的实时协调，解决当前AI在物理系统中缺乏可靠世界模型和泛化能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具（深度学习、生成式AI）在嵌入物理系统时表现不佳，无法在不确定环境下进行长期规划或泛化到未见场景。虽然无线网络可通过感知和通信编排物理智能，但现有架构仅优化吞吐量、延迟和可靠性，无法支持需要共享时空上下文的实时物理AI协调。

Method: 提出HDT-Nets框架，每个全息数字孪生（HDT）作为跨越物理智能体和网络边缘的分层结构，实现本地自主推理与相邻HDT协作。关键技术包括：(1)因果马尔可夫毯确定协调关系并支持反事实推理；(2)主动推理通过最小化预期自由能统一感知、行动和学习；(3)范畴论确保跨异构智能体的语义结构保持；(4)整合信息论量化集体智能和网络智能演化。

Result: HDT-Nets框架实现了实时物理AI推理，全息智能体能够主动推理环境而非被动镜像物理资产，通过形成集体智能单元提高整体性能。各HDT在本地层级自主决策，同时与相邻节点协作，根据信念的认知价值决定传输内容，确保网络智能的有效演化。

Conclusion: HDT-Nets通过整合多种前沿理论，为物理AI协调提供了系统化解决方案。该框架不仅解决了当前AI在物理环境中的根本局限，还建立了可量化的集体智能评估机制，为未来分布式智能系统的网络架构提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Despite advances in artificial intelligence (AI) across multiple sectors, today's AI tools, including deep learning and generative AI, still fail when embedded into physical systems, such as robots and vehicles operating under real-world physical laws. This stems from their inability to maintain reliable world models for long-horizon planning under uncertainty and generalize to unseen scenarios. In this context, wireless networks, through pervasive sensing and communication, can orchestrate physical intelligence. However, current architectures optimize throughput, latency, and reliability and cannot support real-time physical AI coordination, requiring agents to maintain shared spatiotemporal context. To address these challenges, a network of holonic digital twins (HDT-Nets) framework is proposed to deliver real-time physical AI inference through holonic agents that actively reason about their environment rather than passively mirror physical assets. Each HDT is realized as a hierarchical structure spanning the physical agent and network edge, reasoning autonomously at the local level while cooperating with neighboring HDTs to form collectively intelligent units. In HDT-Net, causal Markov blankets spanning sensing, communication, and control determine which agents must coordinate and enable counterfactual reasoning over multi-domain interventions. Active inference within these boundaries unifies perception, action, and learning by minimizing expected free energy while deciding which beliefs to transmit based on their cognitive value to the receiver. Category theory ensures that transmitted beliefs preserve semantic structure across heterogeneous agents with incompatible representations. Finally, integrated information theory quantifies when collective intelligence exceeds independent operation and how network intelligence evolves through coordinated learning and information exchange.

</details>
